Successfully Rushing will give you the room's regular rewards plus a special bonus!=加速成功可以让你获得额外的特别奖励！
Assign Dwellers to production rooms to obtain resources.=将居民分配到生产车间来获得资源。
Rooms run on Power. Dwellers run on Food and Water.=生产车间需要电力，居民需要水和食物。
Dwellers with high Strength perform better in Power rooms.=高力量的居民可以在动力室里发挥更大的作用。
Dwellers with high Perception perform better in Water rooms.=高感知的居民可以在饮水间内发挥更大的作用。
Dwellers with high Agility perform better in Food rooms.=高敏捷的居民可以在食物间内发挥更大的作用。
Build a Science Lab to produce RadAway.=建造一个科学实验室来生产消辐宁。
Upgrading a room will make it more efficient and give you more storage.=升级房间可以提升效率，并且提供更多的储藏空间。
The Weight Room will allow you to train a Dweller's Strength.=举重室可以让你的居民提升力量。
The Armory room will allow you to train a Dweller's Perception.=武器室可以让你的居民提升感知能力。
The Fitness Room will allow you to train a Dweller's Endurance.=健身房可以让你的居民提升耐力。
The Lounge will allow you to train a Dweller's Charisma.=休息室可以让你的居民提升魅力。
The Classroom will allow you to train a Dweller's Intelligence.=教室可以让你的居民提升智力。
The Athletics Room will allow you to train a Dweller's Agility.=体育室可以让你的居民提升敏捷。
The Game Room will allow you to train a Dweller's Luck.=游戏室可以让你的居民提升幸运。
Build Storage Rooms to increase your Vault's Weapon and Outfit holding capacity.=建造储藏室来增加避难所中武器和服装的储藏容量。
There are higher tier versions of the production rooms that are more efficient, but more expensive.=有更高级的生产车间，它们拥有更高的效率但同时造价也更昂贵。
If you are out of building space on a floor, build an elevator to access lower parts of your Vault.=如果你在本层的建筑空间已用完，请建造一个电梯进入下一层避难所。
Build Medbays and Science Labs to increase your limit of Stimpaks and RadAway.=建造药房和科学实验室可以提升治疗针和消辐宁的储量。
Rooms farthest from a power source will shut down first.=距离电力供应源最远端的房间会先行关闭。
The longer a Dweller survives in the Wasteland, the better gear they will find.=居民在废土生存得越久，就能够找到越好的装备。
When a room finishes production, you have a chance to get bonus CAPS based on the Dwellers' Luck.=房间完成生产后，根据居民的幸运，你将有几率获得额外的奖励。
During a Raider attack, flashing resource bars means they are stealing your resources!=在遭遇袭击的时候，资源条在闪动表示有人正在偷窃你的资源！
When a Dweller is assigned to the Radio room it will increase all Vault Dwellers happiness.=分派一个居民到广播室之后，整个避难所居民的愉悦度会提升。
If a room is full of Dwellers, the Dweller with the lowest stat for that room will swap places with the new Dweller.=如果一个房间已经满员，那么该房间里状态最差的居民将会被新来的居民替换掉。
Confused how something works?  Check the VDSG in the Pip-Boy for help.=不知道如何进行一些工作？请查看避难所居民生存指南。
Upgrading your Vault entrance will make it harder for Raiders to break in.=改造避难所入口可以更好地防止掠夺者闯入。
Need extra CAPS? You can sell weapons and outfits from your Vault storage.=需要更多的瓶盖？你可以把避难所里的武器和服装卖掉。
Higher level Dwellers with high SPECIAL have a better chance of surviving in the Wasteland.=等级越高，SPECIAL属性越多的居民将会在废土生存得越久。
Your Dweller does not suffer damage or radiation when coming back from the Wasteland.=从废土回来的时候，居民不会受到伤害或是辐射。
To quickly check on your Dwellers exploring, just tap directly on the Wasteland.=请直接点击废土来查看居民的探索进度。
To quickly check on your Dwellers exploring, just click directly on the Wasteland.=请直接点击废土来查看居民的探索进度。
Raiders can strike any time, be sure to have Dwellers ready and on guard!=掠夺者会在任何时候发动攻击，请让居民做好准备！
Radio rooms attract new Dwellers and improve the happiness in your Vault.=广播室可以吸引新的居民加入，也可以提升避难所的愉悦度。
A nuclear winter is cold, keep warm with Vault-Tec!=核冬天非常寒冷，用避难所科技保持温暖！
Vault-Tec. We'll be there!=避难所科技，随叫随到！
Rooms must be the same level in order to be merged.=相同等级的房间才可以合并。
Vault-Tec rules limit Vault capacity to 200 Dwellers.=避难所规定避难所最多只能拥有200个居民。
Mr. Handy will stay on his assigned floor collecting resources until moved to a different floor.=巧手先生将会一直在指定的楼层搜寻资源，直到被分配到另一层。
Deathclaws are faster, stronger and deadlier than Raiders.=死爪比掠夺者要更加迅捷、强力、致命。
Dwellers with high Endurance suffer less radiation damage in the Wasteland.=高耐力的居民将会受到更少的辐射伤害。
Want to fill your Vault with Ghouls? Check out the upgraded Barbershop!=想要在避难所里到处都是尸鬼吗？去看看新升级过的理发店吧！
Dwellers with high Endurance gain more health when they level up.=高耐力的居民将会在升级时获得更多的生命值。
All SPECIAL stats help your Dwellers fight creatures in the Wasteland.=所有SPECIAL属性都将会为居民抵抗废土生物提供帮助。
Crafting Rare and Legendary items always requires Rare and Legendary Junk, respectively.=制造稀有或是传说物品往往需要稀有和传说级别的垃圾。
Feeling lucky? When you scrap an item, you might get all the Junk from its Recipe.=觉得自己很幸运吗？在分解物品时，你有几率获得左右合成用的垃圾。
Vault-Tec takes some of the Outfits worn by new arrivals and puts them in Lunchboxes.=避难所科技会从新加入的成员身上获取他们的服装并且放到避难所科技午餐盒中。
Don’t forget to pack RadAway on Quests, some of the enemies do radiation damage.=在执行任务前，别忘了带消辐宁，一些敌人会对你的居民造成辐射伤害。
Some enemies on Quests are more powerful and have special attacks, try targeting them first!=一些出现在任务中的敌人非常强力并且能够使用特殊攻击，请尝试先攻击它们！
Dweller health does not replenish over time in a Quest... Be sure to pack tons of Stimpaks before leaving.=在任务过程中，居民的生命值无法自动回复……所以在出发前，请确保能带好大量的治疗针。
Pets equipped to dead Dwellers that are not revived during Quests will return to the Vault.=装备在执行任务途中死亡的居民身上的宠物会自行回到避难所。
Rescued Dwellers will wait outside your Vault when a Quest is completed.=被救援的居民将会于任务完成时在避难所外待命。
Save your Critical Hits for the opportune time to deal the most damage!=请节省你的致命一击，并且将其用在适当的时候以此来打出最大的伤害！
Deathclaws are tough, don’t be a hero, bring your friends.=死爪非常强悍，千万不要逞英雄，带上你的朋友一起去。
Bring Pets on Quests to give Dwellers a damage boost!=在任务途中为居民配备宠物将会获得伤害提升！
Some Quests require Secret Clues to unlock. Loot every container you can!=一些任务需要秘密线索才能够解锁，所以请尽可能地收集那些箱子！
Critical Hits can turn the tide of battle... Use them wisely.=致命一击可以扭转局势……请谨慎使用。
Pet bonuses work on Quests too, don’t forget to bring some furry friends!=宠物的加成对任务也是至关重要，别忘了带上一些毛茸茸的伙伴！
Find a shiny new gun while on a Quest? You can equip it right away!=在任务中找到了一把亮蹭蹭的枪？你可以立即进行装备！
Dwellers that die on a Quest can only be revived once the Quest is over.=在任务中死亡的居民可以在任务结束后复活。

Held my breath long enough to find a supply room with a few things above water.=屏住呼吸很久后在水面上找到了一件补给室，里面有些补给。
I chopped the door to pieces and found a large supply closet.=我把门砍成了碎片，找到了一间巨大的储物柜，里面全是补给品。
I spotted Mirelurks hiding down there, so I turned back and searched the Brewery again.=我发现下面藏着许多泥沼蟹，所以我只能回头继续调查啤酒厂了。
I successfully hid from the Raiders. That was close.=我成功躲过了掠夺者，太险了。
I found a hidden box of supplies!=我找到了一盒藏起来的补给品！
I got the Refrigerator open. There were some supplies inside.=我打开了冰箱，里面有些补给品。
Got the door open and explored the Super Duper Mart. I stocked up on supplies.=把门弄开进大无敌超市探索了一圈，我囤了不少补给品。
The Slaves recognized me as friendly. We chatted for a bit, then they moved on.=奴隶们认为我很友好，我们聊了一会儿，然后他们就继续走了。
I helped a dozen slaves escape. To freedom!=我帮助一批奴隶逃跑了，他们自由了！
I found some unopened crates in a storage closet.=我在一间储藏室里发现了一些没打开过的纸板箱。
I patched up the Slave's Leg. He's back on the run. I wish him the best.=我帮奴隶包扎了一下腿部，他继续踏上了逃亡的旅程，希望他一切顺利。
The Ghoul gave me supplies... and some much needed friendship.=尸鬼给了我一些补给，以及我最需要的……友谊。
We struck a deal, and parted on good terms.=我们完成了一笔交易，然后各自和平地离开了。
I put the Talon Company Mercs at ease. We traded stories, and parted as friends.=我让鹰爪公司佣兵放松，互相交换故事之后友好地分别了。
I helped the Hunter locate and kill the Yao Guai.=我帮猎人定位并宰掉了那头妖怪。
The Drunken Drifter passed out. More for me!=醉汉晕了过去，我还能喝更多！
The Brotherhood Patrol took pity on me and gave me some supplies.=兄弟会侦察队可怜我，施舍了一些补给。
I successfully brought the Lost Farmer to the settlement. He's safe now.=我成功把迷路的农民带到了一处定居点，他现在安全了。
I got them off and went inside to stock up.=我甩掉了他们然后进去收集补给。
They're now as docile as lambs, so I can continue exploring the Cave.=他们现在像小羊羔般温顺，我能继续探索洞穴了。
Got ahold of the reins and stopped the Brahmin! The Merchant then gave me a reward.=抓住了缰绳并把双头牛停了下来！于是商人给了我一些奖赏。
I bumped my foot against something and unearthed it to find a pre-war time capsule!=我的脚踢到了什么东西，于是我把它挖了出来，竟然是一个战前的时间囊！
I grit my teeth and got it before the Lake's radiation made me sick.=我咬紧牙关，在湖中的辐射让我犯恶心之前把它捞了上来。
I managed to avoid all the traps and found a well-stocked storage room.=我成功躲过了所有陷阱，找到了一间塞满补给的储藏室。
They let me in, gave me some food, and let me help myself to their junk pile.=他们让我进去，给了我食物，还让我带上些垃圾，想拿多少拿多少。
They never knew I was there! I even helped myself to some loot.=他们永远不会知道我曾经去过那儿！还带走了些战利品。
I noticed a subtle breeze and followed it to a secret room!=我感觉到有一阵微风，并跟着它来到了一间密室！
Boom! I turned a laser arm into a one-shot door opener. Now to look inside...=砰！我把激光臂做成了只有一发弹夹的开门器。现在让我们看看里面有什么……

Every room in the Vault is linked to one of the SPECIAL stats. The better the Dweller's stat is for that room, the more they influence the room's effects.=每个居民都有属性值，称为SPECIAL

- A high Vault Rating earns you bonus CAPS.=根据你的避难所愉悦度，每天会发送一个奖励。
- Once the child has grown up, you will have a new Dweller.=需要新的居民时，你可以鼓励自己的居民生育。
- Building multiple types of the same room will increase its price.=建造新的起居室以收容新的居民。
- Themes can only be applied to fully merged and upgraded Diners and Living Quarters.=升级房间以增加产量。
- Building MedBays and Science Labs will increase the number of Stimpaks and RadAway you can store.=仓库可以增加你的武器，服装，垃圾和宠物的储存空间。
The higher the SPECIAL stat of a Dweller is, the longer it takes them to train to the next level, but you can use Nuka-Cola Quantum to speed up the training time.=把居民拖到训练室能增加他们的SPECIAL属性。

The PC Settings can be accessed by clicking on the gear icon in the Pip-Boy. \n\nBy default, Fallout Shelter has the following hotkey mappings:\n\nEsc – Settings/Cancel\nF1 – Shop\nF2 – Inventory\nF3 – Survival Guide\nF4 – Objectives\nF5 – Wasteland\nF6 – Help\nB – Build/Upgrade\nSpace – Quest List\nLeft Shift – Clipboard\nLeft Control – Room Action\nTab – Dweller List\n\nMove the camera around by pressing on the arrow keys, WASD keys or clicking and dragging the mouse.\n\nZoom in and out using the mouse scroll wheel or with the ‘Q’ and ‘E’ keys.\n\nYou can adjust the camera zoom and scroll speeds in the Settings menu.\n\nWhen Fallout Shelter is minimized to the taskbar or left unattended for too long, it continues to run for a few minutes before going into sleep mode.  \n\nFallout Shelter automatically and periodically saves your game, but if you want to be sure, click on the Save button in the Settings menu. \n=电脑设置可以在哔哔小子菜单中的齿轮选项中打开。\n\n辐射内置的默认快捷键设置如下：\n\nEsc – 设置/取消\nF1 – 商店\nF2 – 物品栏\nF3 – 生存指南\nF4 – 目标\nF5 – 废土\nF6 – 帮助\nB – 建造/升级\n空格 – 任务列表\n左Shift – 笔记板\n左Ctrl – 房间选项\nTab – 居民列表\n\n按方向键，按WASD键或者点击拖动鼠标都可以移动视角。\n\n使用鼠标滚轮或是‘Q’、‘E’键来进行缩放。\n\n您可以在设置菜单中选择缩放的速度。\n\n在最小化游戏后，游戏仍会运行几分钟时间，之后才会进入睡眠模式。 \n\n游戏进度会自动保存，不过如果您想要确保万无一失，那么请手动保存。\n

Depending on what you ask your Dwellers to do, they can become happy or sad.\n\nKeeping Dwellers happy is very important! A happy Vault runs more efficiently and produces resources faster.\nYou can see a specific Dweller's happiness by checking their profile.\n\nHappiness Icon status:\n\nGREEN: Your Dweller is happy, way to go!\n\nYELLOW: Your Dweller is doing okay, but could be happier.\n\nRED: Your Dweller is sad, cheer them up!=居民会根据你安排给他们的任务产生情绪变化。\n\n保持居民心情愉悦是十分重要的！一间气氛欢快的避难所运作地更有效率，生产资源也会更快。\n你可以通过查看居民的资料来了解他/她的心情。\n\n心情图标状态：\n\n绿色：你的居民很快乐，继续保持！\n\n黄色：你的居民心情不赖，但是你可以让他/她更开心。\n\n红色：你的居民很难过，快想办法让他们高兴起来！

Disasters can randomly strike your Vault at any time! Disasters also occur when you fail a room Rush. Watch out for these possible encounters:\n\nFIRES - Put out fires quickly before they spread to other rooms.\n\nRADROACHES - These pesky critters are hard to get rid of. Take them out before they spread.\n\nRAIDERS - Raiders will rush through your Vault and destroy its resources, stop them quickly! Stronger Raiders will even steal CAPS if you're not careful! Upgrading your Vault Entrance will delay Raiders, giving you time to prepare. Putting guards in your Vault Entrance will also provide a front line of defense against the Raiders. You can loot dead Raiders for CAPS and items!\n\nMOLE RATS – These pests are a nuisance and have a nasty habit of burrowing into your Vault from the dirt and chewing on any room's power cables. Kill them quickly, or they'll drain your Vault of Power!\n\nDEATHCLAWS – Any time you open your Vault Door, you're broadcasting an invitation for these beasties to come to your Vault and destroy your resources. Be sure to have Stimpaks and Weapons at the ready, or they'll tear through your Dwellers like a knife through a Fancy Lad Snack Cake! \n\nFERAL GHOULS - Feral Ghouls run through your Vault, wreaking havoc by dealing both physical and radiation damage to the Dwellers they attack.\n\nRADSCORPIONS – These mutated scorpions are able to burrow and attack random rooms in your Vault. Make sure to kill them quickly as their attacks also do radiation damage!\n\nChildren and Pregnant Women will hide whenever an incident occurs.=你的避难所随时都可能遭受灾害的侵袭！加速失败时也会引发灾难。要小心以下敌人：\n\n火灾 - 在火势蔓延至其他房间之前控制它。\n\n辐射蟑螂 - 这些讨厌的东西很难搞定，在它们泛滥之前解决掉。\n\n掠夺者 - 掠夺者会冲入你的避难所破坏你的资源，尽快阻止他们！更强大的掠夺者甚至会偷走你的瓶盖！加固避难所入口可以拖延他们并给予你足够的准备时间。在你的避难所入口派遣守卫也是一项有效的前线防御措施。你能从死亡的掠夺者身上获取瓶盖和其他物品！\n\n鼹鼠 – 这些小东西十分麻烦，它们会从地下钻入你的避难所并咬坏任何一间房内的电缆。快速杀死它们，不然它们会把你避难所里的电力耗尽！\n\n死爪 – 每当你开启避难所大门，就相当于是在邀请这些怪物进来破坏资源。确保你准备好足够的武器和治疗针，不然它们会切碎你的居民，就像你用刀切蛋糕那样！ \n\n狂尸鬼 – 狂尸鬼会疯狂地掠过你的避难所，并且对它们攻击的居民同时造成物理和辐射伤害。\n\n辐射蝎 – 这些突变的蝎子可以挖掘地洞，并且有可能对避难所的任意房间发动攻击。请在第一时间消灭它们，不然它们就会对你们的居民造成辐射伤害！\n\n孩子和孕妇会在危险灾难发生时躲起来。
Dispatch your Dwellers to scavenge the Wasteland. \n\nOut there they will find CAPS, earn Experience, and bring back Weapons, Outfits and Junk. The further out they go, the better the rewards they'll find, but the harder the encounters will become.\n\n- Drag a Dweller to the Wasteland and equip them with a Weapon, an Outfit, a Pet, RadAway and Stimpaks.\n\n- Send them out exploring, but don't forget to check on their progress.\n\n- Dwellers can be recalled from the Wasteland at any time, and take half as long to return from the Wasteland.\n\n- Make sure you bring them home before they die!\n\n- Dwellers automatically return from the Wasteland if they can't carry any more items.\n\n- Dwellers in the Wasteland may stumble upon a special location. Tap the exclamation point icon to choose between entering this location and continuing to explore the Wasteland.=派你的居民去废土收集物品。\n\n在外面居民能获得瓶盖和经验，还会带回武器、服装和垃圾。他们走得越远，找到的物品也更好，但是遇到的敌人也会更加棘手。\n\n- 将一名居民拖至废土，给其配备武器和服装，治疗针、消辐宁还有宠物。\n\n- 送他们出去探索，但是别忘了检查他们的进程。\n\n- 去探索废土的居民随时都能被召回，回来需要花费一半的时间。\n\n- 确保你能把他们活着带回来！\n\n- 居民拿不下物品时，会自动回到避难所。\n\n- 居民在废土中有时候会发现特殊地点，点击感叹号的图标可以选择是否进入该地点或是继续进行探索。

Quests allow you to explore new locations, meet new Dwellers and collect loot.\n\nBuild the Overseer’s Office room to send teams of Dwellers from your Vault on Quests.\n\nTo access the Quest List, click the map icon from the Overseer’s Office or from the Pip-Boy. The Quest List shows you your available Quests, their rewards, requirements and status. \n\nIn Quests, explore the location by dragging Dwellers to new rooms, or just clicking on the room you want them to travel to.\n\nDwellers in combat fight automatically, but you can make them attack a specific enemy by clicking on a Dweller and then on the enemy. You can also click and hold on a Dweller, then drag the target that appears to the enemy.\n\nAs your Dwellers fight they will build up a Critical hit meter; once ready, a checkmark icon will appear over the enemy that your charged Dweller is attacking; clicking it will let you unleash a deadly Critical Hit attack. \n\nClick on the moving arrows that appear once they are in the center of the reticle, the closer the arrows are to the center, the higher the damage multiplier for your Critical Hit attack. \n\nThe Critical Hit meters fills faster for higher Luck Dwellers and the Critical Hit arrows move slower the higher your Dweller Perception is.\n\nYou can see the mandatory and optional Quest objectives by clicking on the Map Icon in the top right corner.\n\nWhen you’re done exploring, click on the Map Icon to return the Quest team to the Vault.=任务可以让你探索新的地区，发现新的居民以及获得战利品。\n\n建造监管者办公室就可以派遣居民外出执行任务。\n\n在监管者办公室或是哔哔小子菜单点击地图的图标就可以进入任务列表。任务列表中会显示当前可执行的任务，以及任务的奖励和要求。\n\n在任务过程中，拖动居民至新的区域可以进行探索，也可以直接点击你想要探索的区域。\n\n居民会自动进行战斗，但是你可以选择居民以及敌人使其攻击特定的敌人。你也可以直接按住一个居民，然后将其拖动至想要攻击的敌人。\n\n在战斗中，居民们会积攒致命一击能量，致命一击能量一旦积攒完成，便会在敌人头上显示一个图标，点击图标便可以发动致命一击。\n\n点击位于标线中央的箭头，箭头越靠近中央，所造成的致命一击伤害就越高。\n\n高幸运的居民，积攒致命一击能量的速度就越快，光标的移动速度回更慢。\n\n点击右上角的地图按钮，你可以查看强制及可选的任务目标。\n\n完成探索后，点击地图图标让任务小队返回避难所。
Quests allow you to explore new locations, meet new Dwellers and collect loot.\n\nBuild the Overseer’s Office room to send teams of Dwellers from your Vault on Quests.\n\nTo access the Quest List, tap the map icon from the Overseer’s Office or from the Pip-Boy. Survival Mode is for players that want an extra layer of challenge. In this mode the same rules apply as in the main game but there are a few changes.\n\n- Resources drain at a faster rate.\n\n- Incidents do more damage.\n\n- Dwellers can't be revived, whether in the Vault, in the Wasteland or on Quests.\n\n- Objectives are more demanding but give better rewards.\n\n- Quests are more challenging.=生存模式是为那些不满足于当前挑战的玩家准备的。该模式采用了主模式的游戏规则，但是有一些些改变。\n\n- 资源消耗速度更快。\n\n- 危险灾难造成的损害更大。\n\n- 居民无法复活，不论是在避难所、废土还是在任务中。\n\n-目标会有更多的要求，但是奖励也更为丰厚。\n\n-任务会更加具有挑战性。

There are lots of ways you can earn CAPS.\n- Find them in the Wasteland and on Quests\n- Complete Objectives\n- Level up Dwellers\n- Sell items\n- Every Lunchbox contains CAPS\n- Lucky Dwellers earn them occasionally while collecting resources \n\nCAPS can be spent to:\n- Build Rooms and elevators\n- Destroy rocks \n- Upgrade Rooms\n- Revive Dwellers\n- Craft Weapons, Outfits and Themes\n- Customize Dwellers=你可以通过以下方式获得瓶盖。\n- 在废土或是任务中寻找\n- 完成目标\n- 升级居民\n- 售卖物品\n- 每个避难所科技午餐盒都包含瓶盖\n- 幸运的居民偶尔会在收集资源时获得 \n\n瓶盖可用于：\n- 建造房间和电梯\n- 清理巨石 \n- 升级房间\n- 复活居民\n- 制作武器、服装和布局\n- 自定义居民

You can use Junk and pay CAPS to craft items in the Weapon, Outfit and Theme Workshops.\n\nCOMMON, RARE and LEGENDARY Junk is found in Lunchboxes, in the Wasteland and on Quests, and produced by scrapping Weapons and Outfits. Rarer items require rarer Junk.\n\nYou also need an item's Recipe to craft it. Workshops start with some Recipes, but look for others on dead Raiders, in the Wasteland and on Quests.\n\nTheme Recipes come in Fragments. Find these Fragments on Quests or use Nuka-Cola Quantum to complete your Theme Recipes.\n\nClick on a Workshop's Craft button to see the items you can make there and select the one you want. Upgraded Weapon and Outfit Workshops can craft RARE and LEGENDARY items.\n\nAll your Junk is refunded if you cancel the crafting process after it's started, but only part of the CAPS you spent.=你可以在武器、服装和布局工作间使用垃圾和花费瓶盖来制作物品。\n\n普通、稀有和传说级别的垃圾能够在避难所科技午餐盒、废土中、任务以及通过摧毁武器和服装获得。制作更稀有物品需要使用更稀有的垃圾。\n\n制作物品还需要配方。工作间一开始就有一些配方，但是其他配方需要从死掉的掠夺者身上、废土中或是任务获得。\n\n布局配方从碎片而来。在任务中找到这些碎片或者是用量子型核子可乐完成你的布局配方。\n\n点击工作间的制作键查看你能制作的物品，选择你想要制作的物品。升级工作间可以进行稀有以及传说级物品的制作。\n\n取消已经进行的制作会返还全部的垃圾以及一部分瓶盖。

^Your FOOD, WATER and POWER resources are what keeps your Vault going.\n\n- FOOD: Dwellers need to eat to stay healthy. Run low on FOOD and your Dwellers will begin to lose Health.\n\n- WATER: Clean water is hard to come by in the Wasteland. Run low on WATER and your Dwellers will take Radiation damage.\n\n- POWER: Your Vault can't run without POWER. Run low on POWER and your rooms will begin shutting down.\n\nKeep your resources above the required resource lines to keep your Vault happy.\nResources continue to be consumed for a short time after closing the game.$=食物、水源、电力维持避难所运转；资源不足会产生负面效果，退出游戏后资源仍会短时消耗。
